# Command: show icam entries acl module inst

## Syntax
```
show icam entries acl module <module> inst <inst> [ history <num_intervals> ] [ sort { top <top_x> &#124; sort-order { ascending
 &#124; descending } &#124; filter <f_f> [ exact ] &#124; top <top_x> sort-order { ascending &#124; descending } &#124; top <top_x> filter <f_f> [ exact
 ] &#124; top <top_x> sort-order { ascending &#124; descending } filter <f_f> [ exact ] &#124; top <top_x> filter <f_f> [ exact ] sort-order
 { ascending &#124; descending } &#124; sort-order { ascending &#124; descending } top <top_x> &#124; sort-order { ascending &#124; descending } filter
 <f_f> [ exact ] &#124; sort-order { ascending &#124; descending } filter <f_f> [ exact ] top <top_x> &#124; sort-order { ascending &#124; descending
 } top <top_x> filter <f_f> [ exact ] &#124; filter <f_f> [ exact ] top <top_x> &#124; filter <f_f> [ exact ] sort-order { ascending
 &#124; descending } &#124; filter <f_f> [ exact ] top <top_x> sort-order { ascending &#124; descending } &#124; filter <f_f> [ exact ] sort-order
 { ascending &#124; descending } top <top_x> } ] [ __readonly__ <module> <instance> [ <num_intervals> ] [ { TABLE_ACL_entries <Feature>
 <Pkt_Type> <SourceIP_Mask_DestIP_Mask> <Action> <Intf_name> <Stats> [ <Rate> ] } ] ]
```

### Syntax Description

| Parameter | Description |
|-----------|-------------|
| show | Show running system information |
| icam | intelligent CAM |
| entries | TCAM Entries with result and stats |
| acl | ACL entries |
| module | Module Number |
| module | Enter Module Number |
| inst | ASIC/Forwarding Engine Instance Number |
| inst | Enter Instance Number |
| sort | (Optional) Sorted display |
| top | (Optional) Show top x% entries (Default:100%) |

**Command Mode:** /exec

**Source:** b_N9K_Show_Commands_93x_chapter_01001.html
**Tags:** show-mode, security, S-commands
**Command ID:** wp5632210500

# Command: show icam health

## Syntax
```
show icam health [ __readonly__ { <Version> } [ { TABLE_cpu <CpuTypeName> <CpuTypeValue> } ] [ { TABLE_mem <MemTypeName> <MemTypeValue>
 } ] [ { TABLE_ps <PsTypeName> <PsTypeValue> } ] ]
```

### Syntax Description

| Parameter | Description |
|-----------|-------------|
| show | Show running system information |
| icam | iCAM - intelligent CAM |
| health | Health status |
| __readonly__ | (Optional) |
| Version | (Optional) Version of output format |
| TABLE_cpu | (Optional) Table CPU Usage |
| CpuTypeName | (Optional) CPU Usage Type |
| CpuTypeValue | (Optional) CPU Usage Value |
| TABLE_mem | (Optional) Table Memory Usage |
| MemTypeName | (Optional) Memory Usage Type |

**Command Mode:** /exec

**Source:** b_N9K_Show_Commands_93x_chapter_01001.html

# Command: show icam prediction scale

## Syntax
```
show icam prediction scale [ { { l2-switching [ mac-addresses &#124; mst-instances &#124; mst-vports &#124; rpvst-vports &#124; rpvst-vlans &#124;
 total-vlans-x-ports &#124; vlans &#124; infra { mac } &#124; stp { mst-instance &#124; mst-vport &#124; rpvst-vport &#124; rpvst-vlan &#124; isolated-portvlan
 } &#124; vlan { vlan-count } ] } &#124; { multicast-routing [ multicast-routes &#124; igmp-groups &#124; pim-neighbors &#124; outgoing-interfaces &#124;
 routing-forwarding { route-v4 &#124; route-v6 &#124; outgoing-interface } &#124; igmp { group } &#124; pim { neighbor } ] } &#124; { unicast-routing
 [ bfd-sessions &#124; eigrp-routes &#124; ipv4-arp &#124; ipv4-host-routes &#124; ipv6-host-routes &#124; ipv4-isis-routes &#124; ospf-nbr &#124; ospf-lsa &#124;
 ospf-area &#124; ospf-vrf &#124; ospf-passive-intf &#124; bfd { session } &#124; bgp { neighbor } &#124; eigrp { route &#124; neighbor } &#124; hsrp { mac }
 &#124; arp { arp-count } &#124; ipv6-nd { nd-count } &#124; routing { host-route-v4 &#124; host-route-v6 &#124; lpm-route-v4 &#124; lpm-route-v6 } &#124; isis
 { adjacency &#124; bfd-session &#124; route } &#124; ospf { neighbor &#124; lsa &#124; area } &#124; vrf { vrf-count } &#124; vrrp { grp-per-intf } &#124; pbr { seq-per-policy
```

```
show interface <ifrange> counters [ __readonly__ TABLE_interface <interface> [ <overlay_load_interval> ] [ <overlay_rx_ucastpkts>
 ] [ <overlay_rx_ucastbytes> ] [ <overlay_rx_mcastpkts> ] [ <overlay_rx_mcastbytes> ] [ <overlay_rx_pkts> ] [ <overlay_rx_bytes>
 ] [ <overlay_rx_bcastpkts> ] [ <overlay_rx_bcastbytes> ] [ <overlay_rx_bitrate> ] [ <overlay_rx_pktrate> ] [ <overlay_tx_ucastpkts>
 ] [ <overlay_tx_ucastbytes> ] [ <overlay_tx_mcastpkts> ] [ <overlay_tx_mcastbytes> ] [ <overlay_tx_bcastpkts> ] [ <overlay_tx_bcastbytes>
 ] [ <overlay_tx_pkts> ] [ <overlay_tx_bytes> ] [ <overlay_tx_bitrate> ] [ <overlay_tx_pktrate> ] ]
```

### Syntax Description

| Parameter | Description |
|-----------|-------------|
| show | Show running system information |
| interface | Show interface status and information |
| ifrange | Enter interface type and number |
| counters | Show interface counters |
| __readonly__ | (Optional) Read Only |
| interface | (Optional) Interface index |
| TABLE_interface | (Optional) show interface |
| overlay_load_interval | (Optional) Load interval |
| overlay_rx_ucastpkts | (Optional) Received ucast pkts |
| overlay_rx_ucastbytes | (Optional) Received ucast bytes |

**Command Mode:** /exec

**Source:** b_N9K_Show_Commands_93x_chapter_01001.html
**Tags:** show-mode, interface, S-commands
**Command ID:** wp4013362132

# Command: show interface counters

## Syntax
```
show interface <ifrange> counters [ __readonly__ { TABLE_nve_counters <interface> [ <ucast_inbytes> ] [ <ucast_inpkts> ] [
 <ucast_outbytes> ] [ <ucast_outpkts> ] [ <mcast_inbytes> ] [ <mcast_inpkts> ] [ <mcast_outbytes> ] [ <mcast_outpkts> ] } ]
```

### Syntax Description

| Parameter | Description |
|-----------|-------------|
| show | Show running system information |
| interface | Show interface status and information |
| ifrange | Enter interface type and number |
| counters | Show interface counters |
| __readonly__ | (Optional) Read Only |
| interface | (Optional) Interface index |
| TABLE_nve_counters | (Optional) show interface |
| ucast_inbytes | (Optional) ucast bytes input |
| ucast_inpkts | (Optional) ucast packets input |
| ucast_outbytes | (Optional) ucast bytes output |

**Command Mode:** /exec

**Source:** b_N9K_Show_Commands_93x_chapter_01001.html
**Tags:** show-mode, interface, S-commands
**Command ID:** wp3623210115

# Command: show interface counters brief

## Syntax
```
show interface <ifeth_ctr_brf> counters brief [ <counter_val> ] [ __readonly__ TABLE_interface <interface> <eth_inrate1> <eth_inframes1>
 <eth_outrate1> <eth_outframes1> <eth_load_intv1> <eth_inrate2> <eth_inframes2> <eth_outrate2> <eth_outframes2> <eth_load_intv2>
 [ <eth_inrate3> <eth_inframes3> <eth_outrate3> <eth_outframes3> <eth_load_intv3> ] ]
```

### Syntax Description

| Parameter | Description |
|-----------|-------------|
| show | Show running system information |
| interface | Show interface status and information |
| ifeth_ctr_brf | Enter interface type and number in module/slot format |
| counters | Show interface counters |
| brief | Show interface counters in brief |
| counter_val | (Optional) Specify a single load interval id to show the rates |
| __readonly__ | (Optional) Read Only |
| TABLE_interface | (Optional) show interface |
| interface | (Optional) Interface index |
| eth_inrate1 | (Optional) interval 1 input rate in mbps |

**Command Mode:** /exec

**Source:** b_N9K_Show_Commands_93x_chapter_01001.html
**Tags:** show-mode, interface, S-commands
**Command ID:** wp5156043400

| __readonly__ | (Optional) Read Only |
| interface | (Optional) Interface index |
| TABLE_interface | (Optional) show interface |
| eth_inrate1 | (Optional) interval 1 input rate in mbps |
| eth_inframes1 | (Optional) interval 1 input rate in frames (pkts) |

```
 <no_discard_rt_int> [ <passive_dflt> ] [ <bfd_enabled> ] [ <segrt_configured> ] [ <segrt_enabled> ] [ { <srgb_min_label> <srgb_max_label>
```
